# IMPERIA COLD WAR — USA 1970 national programmes and negotiated partnerships.
COLDWAR_USA_legacy_complete_USA_1970_NIX_A0 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_done_NIX_A0 }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_NIX_A0
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_USA_legacy_ready_USA_1970_NIX_A0 = yes }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus USA_1970_NIX_A0"
			add_political_power = 50
			add_stability = -0.01
			set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -5 }
			modify_treasury_effect = yes
			set_temp_variable = { temp_change = 0.02 }
			USA_change_federal_budget_bureaucracy_cost_change = yes
			set_country_flag = USA_1970_nixon_opening_agenda
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_done_NIX_A0
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_NIX_A0
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_USA_early_legacy_bundle_migrate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = USA
			IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_early_legacy_bundles_migrated }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = USA_1970_F7
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_early_root_israel_authorised }
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_done_F7
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_F7 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { federal_budget_bureaucracy_cost = -0.01 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_F7
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = USA_1970_E9
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_early_root_trade_authorised }
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_done_E9
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_E9 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { federal_budget_bureaucracy_cost = -0.02 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_E9
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = USA_1970_NIX_F3
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_early_alliances_authorised }
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_done_NIX_F3
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_NIX_F3 }
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { domestic_foreign_influence_defense = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { federal_budget_export_money_return = -0.01 }
			add_to_variable = { federal_budget_bureaucracy_cost = -0.03 }
			add_to_variable = { federal_budget_personnel_cost = -0.02 }
			add_to_variable = { domestic_offensive_foreign_influence = 0.01 }
		}
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_legacy_pending_NIX_F3
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_early_legacy_bundles_migrated
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
}
